searchText.length) None\n        else findPosition(searchText, target, from + 1, maxIterations - 1)\n      }\n    }\n\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\")\n    val result = val end = start + word.length\n        processToken(word, result.size).foreach { _ =>\n          result += ((word, start, end))\n        }\n        currentPos = end\n      }\n    }\n\n    result.toList\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Return the number of tokens in the text.\n   */\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = {\n    tokenize(text).size\n  }\n}\n\n// structures.\n */\ncase class JsonTokenizer(pretty: Boolean = false) {\n  def tokenize(value: JsonValue): Token = {\n    val jsonStr = if (pretty) {\n      value match {\n        case map: Map[String, n.toString\n    case b: Boolean => b.toString\n    case null => \"null\"\n    case m: Map[String, Any] @unchecked =>\n      m.map { case (k, v) => s\"\"\"\"$k\":${\" \" * indent}$serialize(v, indent +  @tailrec\n    def traverse(current: Any, remaining: List[String]): Option[Any] = remaining match {\n      case Nil => Some(current)\n      case part :: rest =>\n        current match {\n ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Token registry for managing multiple tokenizers.\n */\nclass TokenRegistry[T] {\n  private val registry: mutable.Map[String, Seq[Token]] = mutable.Map.empty\n  private val handlers: mutable.ListBuffer[Token => Option[Token]] = mutable.ListBuffer.empty\n\n  def register(key: String, tokens: Seq[Token]): registry.get(key).map { tokens =>\n      tokens.flatMap { token =>\n        handlers.toSeq.flatMap(_.apply(token)).headOption match {\n          case Some(result) => Some(result)\n          case None }\n}\n\n/**\n * Tokenizer for numeric types with precision handling.\n */\ncase class NumericTokenizer(\n  precision: Int = 6,\n  formatOptions: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case bd: BigDecimal =>\n      val strValue = bd.setScale(precision, BigDecimal.ROUND_HALF_UP).toString\n ']', '{', '}')\n\n  private def processToken(word: String, position: Int): Option[Token] = {\n    var processed = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      processed = processed.filterNot(punctuation.contains)\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      processed = processed.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (processed.length < minLength) {\n      None\n    } else {\n      val }\n  case object BINARY extends TokenType { val value = \"binary\" }\n  case object NULL extends TokenType { val value = \"null\" }\n\n  def fromString(s: String): Option[TokenType] = s match {\n Token(value.format(DateTimeFormatter.ISO_LOCAL_DATE_TIME), TokenType.TEMPORAL)\n    }\n  }\n\n  implicit object ZonedDateTimeTokenizable extends Tokenizable[ZonedDateTime] {\n    def toToken(value: ============================================================================\n// Concrete Tokenizer Implementations\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Tokenizer for string and bytes types.\n */\ncase class StringTokenizer(\n  encoding: String = \"UTF-8\",\n  normalizer: String => String = identity\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case bytes: Array[Byte] =>\n      val strValue = new }\n}\n\n/**\n * Tokenizer for numeric types with precision handling.\n */\ncase class NumericTokenizer(\n  precision: Int = 6,\n  formatOptions: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case bd: BigDecimal =>\n      val strValue = bd.setScale(precision, BigDecimal.ROUND_HALF_UP).toString\n      Token(strValue, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"BigDecimal\"))\n    case d: Double if !d.isNaN && !d.isInfinity =>\n      val strValue = \"%.%df\".format(precision, d)\n      Token(strValue, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Double\"))\n    case f: Float if !f.isNaN && !f.isInfinity =>\n      val strValue = \"%.%df\".format(precision, f)\n      Token(strValue, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> \"Long\"))\n    case other =>\n      Token(other.toString, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> other.getClass.getSimpleName))\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n *
